# US Congress bills. Each bill is a decision that the queue in
# common/scripted_effects/USA_congress_bill_queue.txt activates when its unlock condition holds
# and Congress is free. It is shown only while its vote runs.
#   complete_effect: set the vote variables and starting support, then USA_begin_congress_vote.
#   remove_effect:   USA_record_congress_vote, apply the outcome, then USA_finish_congress_vote.
# New bills also need tokens in common/synchronized_dynamic_tokens/CWIC_US_Congress.txt
# and USA_CONGRESS_SHORT_/USA_CONGRESS_EFFECTS_ keys in US_Congress_Votes_l_english.yml.
CONGRESS_US = {
	The_Admission_of_Alaska_and_Hawaii = {
		allowed = { tag = USA }
		visible = { has_decision = The_Admission_of_Alaska_and_Hawaii }
		available = { always = no }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 0 }
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 50
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation = token:The_Admission_of_Alaska_and_Hawaii }
				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation_short = token:USA_CONGRESS_SHORT_The_Admission_of_Alaska_and_Hawaii }
				set_variable = { usa_current_bill_effects = token:USA_CONGRESS_EFFECTS_The_Admission_of_Alaska_and_Hawaii }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_house_required = 217 }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_senate_required = 0 }	# 0 = simple majority
				set_variable = { bipartisan_value = 0.55 }
				bipartisan_house_calc = yes
				senate_normal_dem_support = yes
				senate_dixiecrat_dem_support = yes
				senate_gop_total_support = yes
				USA_begin_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = USA_CONGRESS_DECISION_VOTE_TT
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_ideas = usa_statehood_expansion
				add_political_power = 30
				463 = { add_core_of = USA }
				629 = { add_core_of = USA }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				USA_record_congress_vote = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { usa_last_bill_result = 1 } }
					country_event = { id = us_congress.100 }
					add_ideas = usa_statehood_expansion
					add_political_power = 30
					463 = { add_core_of = USA }
					629 = { add_core_of = USA }
					set_country_flag = ak_hi_state
				}
				else = {
					country_event = { id = parliament.1 }
				}
				USA_finish_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
	}
	The_Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act = {
		allowed = { tag = USA }
		visible = { has_decision = The_Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act }
		available = { always = no }

		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 50
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {

				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation_short = token:USA_CONGRESS_SHORT_The_Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act }
				set_variable = { usa_current_bill_effects = token:USA_CONGRESS_EFFECTS_The_Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_house_required = 217 }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_senate_required = 0 }	# 0 = simple majority
				set_variable = { bipartisan_value = 0.55 }
				bipartisan_house_calc = yes
				senate_normal_dem_support = yes
				senate_dixiecrat_dem_support = yes
				senate_gop_total_support = yes
				USA_begin_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = USA_CONGRESS_DECISION_VOTE_TT
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_political_power = 30
				add_ideas = Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				USA_record_congress_vote = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { usa_last_bill_result = 1 } }
					country_event = { id = us_congress.100 }
					add_political_power = 30
					add_ideas = Price_Anderson_Nuclear_Industries_Indemnity_Act
				}
				else = {
					country_event = { id = parliament.1 }
				}
				USA_finish_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}

				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation_short = token:USA_CONGRESS_SHORT_The_Mutual_Security_Act }

				set_variable = { usa_vote_house_required = 217 }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_senate_required = 0 }	# 0 = simple majority
				set_variable = { bipartisan_value = 0.55 }
				bipartisan_house_calc = yes
				senate_normal_dem_support = yes
				senate_dixiecrat_dem_support = yes
				senate_gop_moderate_support = yes
				USA_begin_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = USA_CONGRESS_DECISION_VOTE_TT
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_political_power = 30
				every_country = { limit = { OR = { original_tag = COL original_tag = VEN original_tag = ECU original_tag = PRU original_tag = BRA original_tag = ARG original_tag = BOL original_tag = PAR original_tag = URG original_tag = CHL } NOT = { has_government = communism } NOT = { has_government = socialist } NOT = { has_government = maoism } } random_owned_controlled_state = { limit = { industrial_complex > 0 free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 1 include_locked = yes } } add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2 add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 instant_build = yes } } random_owned_controlled_state = { limit = { industrial_complex > 0 free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 1 include_locked = yes } } add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2 add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 instant_build = yes } } }
				add_ideas = usa_mutual_security_act
				set_temp_variable = { money_change_temp = -3.0 }
				cwic_change_money = yes
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				USA_record_congress_vote = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { usa_last_bill_result = 1 } }
					country_event = { id = us_congress.100 }
					add_political_power = 30
					add_ideas = usa_mutual_security_act
					set_temp_variable = { money_change_temp = -3.0 }
					cwic_change_money = yes
					every_country = { limit = { OR = { original_tag = COL original_tag = VEN original_tag = ECU original_tag = PRU original_tag = BRA original_tag = ARG original_tag = BOL original_tag = PAR original_tag = URG original_tag = CHL } NOT = { has_government = communism } NOT = { has_government = socialist } NOT = { has_government = maoism } } random_owned_controlled_state = { limit = { industrial_complex > 0 free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 1 include_locked = yes } } add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2 add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 instant_build = yes } } random_owned_controlled_state = { limit = { industrial_complex > 0 free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 1 include_locked = yes } } add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2 add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 instant_build = yes } } }
				}
				else = {
					country_event = { id = parliament.1 }
				}
				USA_finish_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
	}

		visible = { has_decision = Geoengineering_Research_Act_Vote }
		available = { always = no }
		ai_will_do = { factor = 0 }
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_remove = 50
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation = token:Geoengineering_Research_Act_Vote }
				set_variable = { usa_current_legislation_short = token:USA_CONGRESS_SHORT_Geoengineering_Research_Act_Vote }
				set_variable = { usa_current_bill_effects = token:USA_CONGRESS_EFFECTS_Geoengineering_Research_Act_Vote }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_house_required = 217 }
				set_variable = { usa_vote_senate_required = 0 }	# 0 = simple majority
				set_variable = { bipartisan_value = 0.45 }
				bipartisan_house_calc = yes
				senate_gop_total_support = yes
				USA_begin_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = USA_CONGRESS_DECISION_VOTE_TT
			effect_tooltip = {
				add_political_power = 30
				add_ideas = Geoengineering_Research_Act
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				USA_record_congress_vote = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { usa_last_bill_result = 1 } }
					country_event = { id = us_congress.100 }
					add_political_power = 30
					add_ideas = Geoengineering_Research_Act
				}
				else = {
					country_event = { id = parliament.1 }
				}
				USA_finish_congress_vote = yes
			}
		}
	}
